Can we optimize true max with L-BFGS by smoothing λ high? We'll do:
```
def optimize_smooth(q0, n, deadline, ...):
    z = np.log(q0 + eps); z-=z.mean()
    best = q0, score
    for lamb in [50,100,200,500,1000,2000,4000]:
       for attempt in range(2):
          res = minimize(fun, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':...,'maxfun':...,'ftol':...,'gtol':...})
          q = softmax(z)
          score = exact_fast(q); update best.
          if time > deadline break
          # maybe perturb z with small noise
    return best
```

Algorithm coordinate descent:
```
a normalized sum=1
b = conv(a)
for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
  improved=False
  for i in range(n):
    x_old=a[i]; S0=1-x_old
    if S0 <= eps: set a[i]=1-eps; normalize? continue
    C = b / S0^2
    A = C.copy(); B = -2*C
    t_idx = i + np.arange(n)
    mask = np.ones(n,bool); mask[i]=False
    # cross contributions for j != i:
    # C[t_idx[mask]] -= 2*x_old*a[j[mask]]/S0^2
    # B[t_idx[mask]] += 2*a[j[mask]]/S0
    C[t_idx[mask]] -= (2*x_old*a[mask])/S0^2
    B[t_idx[mask]] += (2*a[mask])/S0
    diag=2*i
    if diag<L:
       C[diag] -= x_old*x_old/S0^2
       A[diag] += 1
    # Minimize f
    x_new = minimize_max_quad(A,B,C, x_old, time)
    if x_new < 0: x_new=0; if >1: x_new=1
    a[i]=x_new
    # update b = A*x_new^2 + B*x_new + C
    b = A*x_new*x_new + B*x_new + C
    if b.max() < best_m - tol: improved=True
  a[a<mincut]=mincut; normalize
  b=conv_fast(a) # occasionally to correct
```

    # A,B,C arrays length L, A>=0

```
gr = (np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
a=0.0; b=1.0
c=b-gr*(b-a); d=a+gr*(b-a)
fc=f(c); fd=f(d)
for _ in range(40):
   if fc < fd:
      b=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=b-gr*(b-a); fc=f(c)
   else:
      a=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=a+gr*(b-a); fd=f(d)
   # time check optional
x=(a+b)/2
# Also check boundary near 0,1 and maybe x0? Actually golden may not evaluate boundaries; convex min can be at boundary. Check f(0), f(1), f(x) and return argmin.

Wait in coordinate update, we defined new vector with a_i=x and all other entries scaled to keep sum=1: a_j_new = a_j_old * (1-x)/(1-x_old). This is not just setting a[i]=x and leaving others unchanged! Very important. In our coordinate step, we assumed others scale. If we implement a[i]=x_new and b update using P_t (which represents scaled others), then the actual a vector for other coordinates should also be scaled! Because to keep sum=1, changing a_i requires scaling all other coordinates. In many coordinate methods, one changes a_i and adjusts a single other coordinate or normalizes entire vector. Normalizing entire vector after setting a_i to x is equivalent to scaling all others by 1/(sum_old - old + x) where sum_old=1, new sum=1 - old + x. But our scaling factor was (1-x)/(1-old), not 1/(1-old+x). Are they different? Let's check: If start sum=1. Set a_i=x, leave others, sum=1-old+x. Normalize: factor =1/(1-old+x). Our desired factor = (1-x)/(1-old). Which one corresponds to moving along coordinate with sum=1 while keeping ratios of others fixed? There are two ways: Old others ratio fixed; x arbitrary. To sum 1, others factor = (1-x)/(1-old). That is what polynomial used. If we instead set x and normalize, others factor =1/(1-old+x), different. We need update a_j accordingly. We can update a[1:] = a[1:] * factor. That means each coordinate update rescales all others. This is coordinate direction in simplex: choose new value for coordinate i, keep relative weights of others. That's valid and convex. It may be more costly to update entire a each coordinate O(n), but O(n) total per coordinate (512*512=262k per sweep) fine. But if we do for each i, after many coordinates, a ratios change in complicated way. Need implement correctly.

Option simpler: Use normalization after setting a_i=x (others unchanged) and recompute b by conv_fast from scratch occasionally. But coordinate f would not match previous; still might be descent? If we set a_i=x and normalize, new vector: a_i'=x/(1-old+x), others'=a_j/(1-old+x). This also keeps ratios of others, different x' in original unnormalized. The polynomial for unnormalized x_unscaled = x_specific with factor? Let unnormalized variable y=a_i_new_unscaled, others unchanged; then normalize by S=1-old+y. Score invariant, so we can minimize max of normalized b = (base + ...)/(S^2). This is also convex? b normalized is quadratic over quadratic; not necessarily convex. So coordinate convex property lost. Thus use scale-others factor (1-x)/(1-old) and update a_j accordingly. Good.

Coordinate update procedure:
- Current a sum=1.
- For coordinate i, x_old=a[i], S0=1-x_old.
- For candidate x∈[0,1], new vector: a'_i=x, a'_j = a_j*(1-x)/S0 for j≠i. This can be represented on simplex along geodesic keeping others ratios.
- To update after optimum x_new:
```
factor = (1 - x_new) / S0
a *= factor  # scales all including i? a[i] old * factor = x_old*factor, not x_new. Need set after scaling others.
a[:-? i] = ...
```

```
a[i] = 0.0
a *= factor  # now a[i]=0, others scaled
a[i] = x_new
```
But careful if S0 small. This updates all entries O(n) per coordinate. Then sum = x_new + S0*factor =1.
- Update b = P_t(x_new) directly; no need conv. But due to repeated updates and scaling, b should be consistent with a. We can recompute b from a occasionally (after each sweep or every few coordinates) using efficient conv_fast to correct numerical drift. Within sweep, b updated by P_t exact if A,B,C correct.

This coordinate step code:
```
def _coord_min_step(a,b,i,n,L):
    x0 = a[i]
    S0 = 1.0 - x0
    if S0 <= 1e-14:
       return None, x0
    S2 = S0*S0
    C = np.empty? C = b / S2 (alloc)
    A = C.copy()
    # cross arrays
    cross = 2.0*x0*a
    cross[i] = 0.0
    cross /= S2
    C[i:i+n] -= cross
    B = -2.0*C
    add = 2.0*a
    add[i] = 0.0
    B[i:i+n] += add / S0
    diag=2*i
    if diag < L:
       C[diag] -= x0*x0/S2
       A[diag] += 1.0
    # Due to rounding, A may be negative? A=C but C nonnegative; clip small
    A = np.maximum(A, 0.0) # But clipping changes polynomial? If negative tiny ok. Might hide.
    x_new = _min_unimodal(A,B,C, x0)
    return x_new, maybe f_new
```

LP implementation details:
- Use current normalized q (sum=1). m=b.max. Set M = m*(1+1e-9).
- Build A: rows t, cols i: A[t,i]=q_{t-i} if valid. This is same as convolution matrix. We can build dense using slices of q reversed? Let's derive for speed.
For t=0..n-1 (ascending length): valid i=0..t, A[t,i]=q[t-i]. That's q[t],q[t-1],...,q[0]. For t=n-1..2n-2: valid i=t-n+1..n-1, A[t,i]=q[t-i]. We can build zeros dense A shape (2n-1,n). For each row t, slice A[t, start:end] = q[t:end?][::-1]? Parent code uses qr=q[::-1]. For t=0..n-1: A[t,0:t+1]=q[t::-1]. For t=n-1..2n-2: start=t-n+1, length=n-start, A[t,start:n]=q[t-start::-1]? Simpler:
```
A=np.zeros((L,n))
qr=q[::-1]
for t in range(L):

  Lq=end-start+1
  # Need q[t-i] for i=start..end. Let values q[t-start], q[t-start-1],... q[t-end]. That's reversed slice q[t-start::-1] length Lq.
  A[t,start:end+1]=q[t-start::-1]
```

`consider` implementation:
```
def _consider(seq):
    nonlocal best_list,best_score
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
       if arr.size==0: return
       arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr)&(arr>0),arr,0.0)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s<=0 or not finite: return
       arr/=s
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       # maybe ensure sum >=0.01 after clip (yes)
       # exact evaluate
       val=evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
       if finite and val<best_score:
           best_score=val; best_list=arr.tolist()
    except: pass
```
Calling tolist for large n many times maybe overhead but okay if not every sweep. We can evaluate exact after each major improvement (when internal m < best_internal - 1e-8) but best_score exact. It can be called every few seconds. For n=2048, tolist 2048 and direct conv maybe fine.

return 2*len(a)*b.max()/s/s
```

Coordinate descent function:
```
def _coordinate_decent(a, deadline, reserve=20.0, max_sweeps=10000, golden_iters=None, restart_perturb=False, rng=None, verbose=False):
    a=_normalize(a.copy())
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; range_n=np.arange(n)
    if golden_iters is None: golden_iters = 45 if n<=512 else (38 if n<=1024 else 32)
    b=_convolve(a)
    m=float(b.max()); best_a=a.copy(); best_m=m
    add_buf = np.empty(n)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
       sweep_m = m
       # coordinate order
       order = range_n or random? We'll accept order list maybe.
       for i in order:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
           x0 = float(a[i])
           S0 = 1.0 - x0
           if S0 <= 1e-12:
               # try reset coordinate?
               continue
           S2 = S0*S0
           C = b / S2
           add = add_buf  # maybe allocate? We can allocate add = 2*a each coordinate. To reuse, add_buf[:] = 2*a; add_buf[i]=0
           add_buf[:] = 2.0*a
           add_buf[i]=0.0
           invS0 = 1.0/S0
           # subtract cross from C
           C[i:i+n] -= (x0*S2inv? actually x0/S2) * add_buf
           diag = 2*i
           if diag < L:
               C[diag] -= x0*x0/S2
           # clip tiny negative
           # Since C = base/S2; ensure nonnegative
           if C.min() < 0.0:
               C += -C.min()? Better clip: np.maximum(C,0,out=C). But if add? Use out.
           # Golden
           x_new = _min_golden_direct(C, add_buf, diag, S0, x0, golden_iters, maybe time_check)
           if not finite: continue
           f_new = _eval_direct(C,add_buf,diag,S0,x_new) # maybe computed in min returns best_val
           if f_new < m - 1e-15:
               factor=(1.0-x_new)/S0
               a[i]=0.0
               a *= factor
               a[i]=x_new
               # b from formula
               sc0 = (1.0-x_new)*(1.0-x_new)
               sc1 = x_new*(1.0-x_new)*invS0
               b = sc0*C
               b[i:i+n] += sc1*add_buf
               if diag < L: b[diag] += x_new*x_new
               # b maybe tiny negative; no
               m = float(b.max())
               if m < best_m - 1e-14:
                   best_m = m; best_a = a.copy()
           # else no update; b remains
       # end order
       # recompute b after sweep to correct drift
       a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12); a/=a.sum()
       b = _convolve(a)
       m = float(b.max())
       if m < best_m - 1e-14:
           best_m=m; best_a=a.copy()
       # convergence check
       if best_m - m < 1e-12? Actually if improvement this sweep small.
       sweep_improvement = sweep_m - m
       if sweep_improvement < 1e-12 and sweep > 3:
           # maybe try a perturbation if allowed
           if rng is not None and do_perturb:
               ...
           else:
               break
    return best_a, best_m*2*n? if best_a normalized
```
But `_min_golden_direct` needs C and add; after golden, if we update b, we need C,add still. Golden can return x_new and f_new. It should not modify C/add.

def _eval_direct(C, add, diag, S0, x):
    sc0=(1-x)*(1-x)
    val = C*sc0
    sc1 = x*(1-x)/S0
    off = x? Actually add already 2a_j. b contribution = add*(x*(1-x)/S0). Yes.
    if n: val[i:i+n] += sc1*add
    if diag>=0: val[diag] += x*x
    return val.max()
```

```
def _eval(C, add, start, n, diag, S0, x):
    one_m = 1.0-x
    val = C*(one_m*one_m)
    if sc1: val[start:start+n] += (sc1*add)
    if diag >=0: val[diag] += x*x
    return float(np.max(val))

Golden function:
```
def _min_golden(C, add, start, n, diag, S0, x0, iters):
    def ev(x): ...
    x0=float(x0)
    f0=ev(x0)
    f00=ev(0.0); f11=ev(1.0)
    best_x = x0; best_f=f0
    if f00<best_f: best_x=0;...
    if f11<best_f: best_x=1;...
    gr=(math.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    a=0.0; b=1.0; c=b-gr; d=a+gr
    fc=ev(c); fd=ev(d)
    update best with c,d
    for _ in range(iters):
       if fc < fd:
          b=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=b-gr*(b-a); fc=ev(c)
       else:
          a=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=a+gr*(b-a); fd=ev(d)
       update best with new point
    # maybe midpoint
    xm=0.5*(a+b); fx=ev(xm); update
    return best_x, best_f
```
The current x0 may be not exactly in interval? It is. This may evaluate x0 again but okay.

Could reduce evals by using `iters` and time. Since `ev` allocs val length L; for many golden, heavy. Maybe we can use a faster line minimization exploiting convex and quadratics? But okay.

Can we compute `ev(x)` without allocating full val? We can compute max of two parts:
- max over offset slice: max_i ( (1-x)^2 C[start+i] + sc1 add[i] ) plus diag x2 if diag in slice (it is at offset i). Since diag = 2i, which is inside slice at offset i. Add diag x2 to that element.
- max outside slice: max C * (1-x)^2 = (1-x)^2 * max(C_outside).
Thus we can avoid allocating val length L! Compute max outside once per coordinate (over C excluding slice) and inside slice vectorized length n. For each x:
```
sc0=(1-x)^2; sc1=x*(1-x)/S0
inside = sc0*C[start:start+n] + sc1*add
if diag>=0: inside[start+i? diag-start=i] += x*x   # index i in slice
m_in = inside.max()
m_out = outside_max * sc0
return max(m_in, m_out)
```
This allocates only length n (same as add) not L. Outside max: indices [0,start) and [start+n,L). If start=0, first empty; start+n <= L. We can compute `outside_max = max(C[:start].max(), C[start+n:].max())` once per coordinate (C doesn't change during golden). This is O(L) once per coordinate, okay. Then each golden eval uses O(n) slice (n=len add) rather than L (2n-1), about half. Good. And no full val allocation.

Let's verify: C base length L. For t in slice [start,start+n), contribution = sc0 C_t + sc1 add_{t-start}. For all t outside slice, add=0 and diag? Diag=2i is in slice at offset i (since start=i, diag-start=i). Only t in slice have add; diag also in slice. So outside just sc0*C_outside. Good.

This is much faster. For coordinate i near middle, outside has ~n length too; but we compute max outside once. Inside eval length n. So each eval ops ~n instead of L ~2n, plus no C*sc0 for outside. Great.

```
def _resample(a,n):
  if a.size==n: return normalize(a)
  x0=np.linspace(0,1,a.size,endpoint=False)
  x1=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
  return np.interp(x1,x0,a,period=?)
```
Periodic endpoint? Linear convolution endpoints matter; periodic interpolation may wrap. Maybe use endpoint=False periodic for smooth. Use `np.interp(x1, x0, a, left=a[-1], right=a[0])` for periodic. Or simple linear endpoint=True. We can use periodic to avoid edges. Normalize.

Random starts for larger n:
```
def _random_smooth(n,rng,alpha=1):
 t=np.arange(n)
 z=np.zeros(n)
 for k in range(1, min(8,n//4)+1):
   z += (0.25/k)*(rng.normal()*np.cos(2*pi*k*t/n)+rng.normal()*np.sin(...))
 a=np.exp(z - z.max()); a/=a.sum(); return a

def _lp_improve(q, deadline, max_steps=10):
 q=normalize
 b=conv; m=b.max(); best=q
 for step in range(max_steps):
   if time > deadline - reserve: break
   M=(1+1e-8)*m + 1e-12
   A=_build_conv_matrix(q)
   try linprog with time_limit = min(5, remaining/4)
   if not success/f: break
   g=res.x sanitize normalize
   # try blends
   cand_best=q; cand_sc=fast_score(q)
   for t in [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
      cand=(1-t)q+t*g; normalize; sc=2n*max(conv(cand))
      if sc < cand_sc: update
   if cand_sc < sc_best - 1e-12:
      best=cand_best; q=cand_best; m=cand_best.max/n?; 
   else: break (or perturb g? no)
   # optional run few coordinate sweeps? Not inside; outer will.
 return best
```

Let's design `_smooth_polish(q, deadline, reserve)`:
```
def _smooth_polish(q, deadline, reserve=30):
  n=q.size; range
  best_q=q; best_m=fast_m(q)
  z0=np.log(np.maximum(q,1e-300)); z0-=z0.mean()
  for lamb in [50, 100, 200, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000]:
     if time > deadline-reserve: break
     z = z0.copy()
     # maybe reset z0 from best after each λ?
     def fun(z):
        zc = z - np.mean(z)
        e = np.exp(zc)
        a = e/e.sum()
        b = np.convolve(a,a)
        bm = b.max()
        shift = lamb*(b-bm)
        ex = np.exp(shift)
        Z = ex.sum()
        f = bm + np.log(Z)/lamb
        w = ex/Z
        # H w a: conv(w, a[::-1])[n-1:2n-1]
        h = np.convolve(w, a[::-1], mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
        grad_a = 2.0*h
        A = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
        grad_z = a*(grad_a - A)
        # remove constant comp
        grad_z -= grad_z.mean()
        return f, grad_z
     # maxiter maybe 60 for low λ, 40 high
     try minimize(method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':80,'maxfun':300,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':20})
     except: break
     z = res.x if finite else z
     z0 = z - z.mean()
     e=np.exp(z0 - max); a=Normalize; score=2n*max(conv(a)); update best
  return best
```
But gradient of logsumexp F: F=bm + log(sum exp(λ(b-bm)))/λ. Derivative of bm term? We used weights derivative, but the stable shift bmax introduces derivative of bmax? Let's derive stable F = (1/λ) log ∑ exp(λ b). If we compute bm + (1/λ) log ∑ exp(λ(b-bm)), derivative wrt a is (∑ exp ∂b/∂a) / ∑exp = w·∂b, not including derivative of bm separately because algebra cancels. Let's verify: f = (1/λ) log( e^{λbm} S ) = bm + (1/λ) log S. df = dbm + (1/λ)(1/S)∑ e^{λ(b-bm)} (∂b - dbm). The total coefficient for ∂b_t: dbm cancels for max t? The stable expression's derivative equals weighted ∂b with weights exp(λb_t)/∑exp(λb_t). So our gradient w·∂b correct; no need account bm derivative. Good.

The gradient `h` from conv of w and reversed a: Need confirm h_i = ∑_t w_t a_{t-i}. The derivative ∂b_t/∂a_i = 2 a_{t-i}. Thus grad_i = 2 ∑_t w_t a_{t-i} = 2h_i. Our conv gives h_i? For w length L and a reversed, slice gave ∑ a_j w_{i+j}. Is that ∑_t w_t a_{t-i}? Let j=t-i => t=i+j, sum_j w_{i+j} a_j. Yes h. Good.

def _coord_step(a,b,i,n,L,grid,refine,iters,):
 x0=a[i]; S0=1-x0
 if S0<=1e-12: return None
 S2=S0*S2
 C=b/S2
 add=2*a; add[i]=0
 C[i:i+n] -= (x0/S2)*add
 diag=2*i
 C[diag] -= x0*x0/S2
 # clip C negatives

 if not finite: return None
 f_old=float(b.max())
 if f_new < f_old - 1e-15:
   ... update a,b
   return True
 return False
```

Let's code `_coordinate_decent` carefully:
```
def _coordinate_decent(a, deadline, reserve=30.0, max_sweeps=10000, grid=None, refine=None, iters=None, rng=None, perturb=False, perturb_scales=(0.05,), verbose=False):
    a = np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a[-?]; n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    a = _normalize(a)
    b = np.convolve(a,a)
    m = float(b.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=m
    if grid is None: ...
    rng = rng or default_rng(0)
    no_improve=0
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
       sweep_m = m
       order = np.argsort(np.random.random(n))? If random each sweep maybe slower. Use forward for deterministic; optional random order? Since coordinate exact, order shouldn't matter at convergence but can affect escape if approximate grid. Use forward then backward maybe. Could use random order for each sweep with rng to identify local. But coordinate min same; approximate may benefit. Use a list `orders`? Simpler: sweep parity forward/reverse.
       idxs = range(n) if sweep%2==0 else range(n-1,-1,-1)
       for i in idxs:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
          x0=float(a[i]); S0=1.0-x0
          if S0 <= 1e-9: continue
          S2=S0*S0
          C = b / S2
          add = 2.0*a
          add[i] = 0.0
          C[i:i+n] -= (x0/S2) * add
          diag=2*i
          C[diag] -= x0*x0/S2
          # clip
          mn = C.min()
          if mn < 0.0:
              C += -mn? Clipping with np.maximum(C,0,out=C) maybe less shift. Use out.
              np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
          # outside max
          outmax=0.0
          if i>0:
             om=np.max(C[:i]); if om>outmax: outmax=om
          end=i+n
          if end<L:
             om=np.max(C[end:]); ...
          x_new, f_new = _min_coord(C, add, i, S0, x0, outmax, grid, refine, iters)
          f_old=m
          if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < f_old - 1e-13:
              factor = (1.0-x_new)/S0
              a[i]=0.0
              a *= factor
              a[i]=x_new
              sc0=(1.0-x_new)**2
              sc1=x_new*(1.0-x_new)/S0
              b = C*sc0
              b[i:i+n] += sc1*add
              b[diag] += x_new*x_new
              # tiny negative clip? no, but maybe b has small neg; max okay. Could `np.maximum(b,0,out=b)`
              m = float(b.max())
              if m < best_m - 1e-14:
                  best_m=m; best_a=a.copy()
       # end sweep
       # recompute and normalize
       a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
       a /= a.sum()
       b = np.convolve(a,a)
       m = float(b.max())
       if m < best_m - 1e-14:
          best_m=m; best_a=a.copy()
       # if perturbed current not best? We maintain best separate. For convergence, if current m not improving best? Could use best_m - m.
       if best_m - m < 1e-12 and (sweep>5):
          no_improve +=1
       else:
          no_improve=0
       if no_improve >= 3:
          if rng is not None and perturb and sweep>10:
              # perturb best or current? Perturb current (a) small and continue
              z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) + 0.03*rng.normal(size=n)
              z-=z.mean()
              ap=np.exp(z); ap/=ap.sum()
              # only accept if score not too bad? set current=ap
              a=_normalize(ap); b=np.convolve(a,a); m=float(b.max())
              if m < best_m * 1.05: # allow some worsening
                 no_improve=0
              else:
                 break
          else:
              break
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
    return best_a, 2*n*best_m
```
But `no_improve` based on `best_m - m`: If current a equals best_m and stuck, no_improve. If perturb, current may worse; best_m unchanged. If no_improve reaches and perturb too bad, break. Good.

Now, `_min_coord` implementation detail: It needs Cslice, add, i_in_slice, outmax. It will evaluate grid vector. We can use local scalar `ev` that refers to Cslice, add, i, outmax. To avoid repeated attribute lookups, assign locals.

```
def _min_coord(C, add, i, S0, x0, outmax, grid, refine, iters):
    n = add.shape[0]
    Cslice = C[i:i+n]
    # scalar ev
    def ev(x):
        o = 1.0 - x
        sc0 = o*o
        arr = Cslice * sc0
        sc1 = x*o/S0
        if sc1 != 0.0:
            arr += sc1 * add  # This modifies arr (alloc? Cslice*sc0 returns new array, in-place ok)
        # if sc1 zero, arr is new? Cslice*sc0 creates new; add still possible.
        arr[i] += x*x
        vi = float(max(arr.max(), outmax*sc0))
        return vi
    # grid
    xs = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,grid)
    sc0 = (1.0-xs)**2
    sc1 = xs*(1.0-xs)/S0
    tmp = sc0[:,None]*Cslice[None,:] + sc1[:,None]*add[None,:]
    tmp[:,i] += xs*xs
    vals_in = tmp.max(axis=1)
    vals = np.maximum(vals_in, outmax*sc0)
    # best initial
    best_x = x0 if 0<=x0<=1 else 0.5
    best_f = min(ev(best_x), vals.min()?) But vals points correspond xs; use idx_min.
    idx_min = int(np.argmin(vals))
    if vals[idx_min] < best_f: best_x=xs[idx_min]; best_f=vals[idx_min]
    # candidates
    cand_idxs = {idx_min, 0, grid-1}
    # x0 cell:
    cell = int(np.clip(x0*(grid-1),0,grid-1))
    for d in range(-2,3): if 0<=cell+d<grid: add
    # top low vals
    if refine >0:
       k = refine+3 maybe
       order = np.argsort(vals)
       for idx in order[:refine+2]: cand_idxs.add(idx)
    # For each idx, interval:
    intervals=[]
    for idx in cand_idxs:
       lo = 0.0 if idx==0 else xs[idx-1]
       hi = 1.0 if idx==grid-1 else xs[idx+1]
       # For boundary, maybe [0,xs[1]] and [xs[-2],1]
       if idx==0: hi=xs[1]
       elif idx==grid-1: lo=xs[-2]
       else: lo=xs[idx-1]; hi=xs[idx+1]
       # avoid tiny intervals: if hi-lo < 1e-8: include midpoint
       intervals.append((lo,hi))
    # Deduplicate intervals? Could just run all; count <=10.
    for lo,hi in intervals:
       if hi-lo < 1e-6: evaluate mid
       else:
          # maybe do local grid 5 then golden best
          # Use golden with 20 iterations
          xg, fg = _golden_interval(ev, lo, hi, iters)
          if fg < best_f - 1e-15: best_x= xg; best_f=fg
    # Also evaluate a few fixed fractions? maybe 1/3,2/3 to handle W minima if grid missed? Add to candidates intervals around 1/3,2/3? We can simply evaluate ev(0.25,0.5,0.75) and intervals around? Since W minima can be at 1/3,2/3; grid likely. But add fixed points to be safe.
    for xp in (0.1,0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75,0.9): update and maybe local interval around xp? We can just evaluate points and if good start golden? Extra cost small.
```
Maybe simpler: use global grid and local golden on top 5 cells; also include x0. Fixed fractions not needed. But for robustness, evaluate boundaries and x0. Good.

Golden interval:
```
def _golden_interval(ev, lo, hi, iters):
    if hi<=lo: x=lo; return x, ev(x)
    gr=(sqrt5-1)/2
    a=lo; b=hi
    c=b-gr*(b-a); d=a+gr*(b-a)
    fc=ev(c); fd=ev(d)
    # Also evaluate endpoints? local min could be at endpoint; include:
    fo=ev(a); fp=ev(b); best among
    for _ in range(iters):
      if fc < fd:
         b=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=b-gr*(b-a); fc=ev(c)
      else:
         a=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=a+gr*(b-a); fd=ev(d)
      # update best with c/d maybe
    x=0.5*(a+b); fx=ev(x)
    return min among all
```

def _resample(a,n):
   a=_normalize(a)
   if a.size==n: return a.copy()
   if a.size==1: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   # periodic interpolation
   x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size,endpoint=False)
   x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
   out=np.interp(x1,x0,a,period=1.0) # np.interp has period param? Actually numpy.interp(x, xp, fp, period=None). If period=1.0, xp in [0,1) and periodic. Good.
   out=np.maximum(out,0)
   return _normalize(out)
```
`np.interp` period introduced? It has `period` parameter since? I think `np.interp(x, xp, fp, left=None, right=None, period=None)`. Yes. If not, fallback. We can use simple: `np.interp(x1 % 1.0, x0, a)` with `left`, but at 0 okay. `x1` 0..1. For `x1` close 1, interp to last. Could wrap by appending first point: xp2=np.concatenate([x0,[1.0]]), fp2=np.concatenate([a,[a[0]]]); out=np.interp(x1,xp2,fp2). This is robust. Use that.

def _random_smooth(n, rng, scale=0.3):
    t=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    maxk=min(20,n//2)
    for k in range(1,maxk+1):
        amp = scale/(k*0.7+1.0)
        z += amp*(rng.normal()*np.cos(2*pi*k*t/n)+rng.normal()*np.sin(...))
    z += 0.2*rng.normal(size=n) / np.sqrt(n)? maybe small
    a = np.exp(z - np.max(z)); a/=a.sum()
    return a
```

Let's examine if our coordinate f formula with C and add correctly accounts for base scaling after x update. Suppose old a sum=1. New vector: a_i=x, a_j = a_j old*(1-x)/(1-x0). The convolution coefficient b_t_new = Sum_{p+q=t} new_p new_q. We split into terms involving i and not. For t where p=i or q=i: exactly one j=t-i. If j valid. If j=i (t=2i), term x^2. If j valid and j≠i, terms p=i,q=j and p=j,q=i total 2 x new_j = 2x a_j (1-x)/S0. For all other pairs (p,q neither i), new_p new_q = a_p a_q ((1-x)^2/S0^2) = base_t * scale. base_t is original b_t minus contributions involving i (which for diagonal is ai^2, nondiag 2ai aj). So C_t = base_t/S0^2 for all t; add_t = 2a_j for t in slice nondiag, 0 otherwise. Then b_new = sc0*C + sc1*add + x^2 I_diag. Our C computed as b/S2, subtract cross and diag, yes. Good.

Now, if x=1, S_other=0, factor=0. C finite? S0 old maybe not 1, sc0=0, sc1=0, diag=1. Good.

If x=0, new_i=0, others scale 1/S0. C = old b minus contributions /S0^2; sc0=1. b_new = base/S0^2. This is conv of normalized others. Good.

Now, local f could be negative? C nonnegative, add nonnegative, x^2 nonnegative for x∈[0,1], sc0>=0, sc1>=0. So f nonnegative. Good.

Now, when `C = b/S2` and subtract contributions, due to b from previous formula maybe has tiny negative, C min <0; clip. Add nonnegative. Good.

Now, exact b after update: We compute `b = C*sc0; b[i:i+n] += sc1*add; b[diag] += x2`. Since C has been clipped, b might be slightly less than true conv. But negligible. At sweep end recompute.

Now, consider if `a` contains zeros; `add` at zero okay. Coordinate x0=0: S0=1, C=b, add zero for i? `C[i:i+n] -= 0`, diag subtract 0. f(x) for turning on coordinate from 0. Global grid can choose x>0 if reduces max (unlikely if coordinate zero and current max maybe high? It might add to reduce? Could). Good.

Now, floor after sweep `a=np.maximum(a,1e-10)` may change objective; recompute. This might prevent getting exact zeros that could be optimal? If optimal has zero, floor adds tiny mass, score change tiny. Fine.

Now, possible improvement: Use `np.roll` shifts of parent before coordinate. If parent has endpoints optimized, shifts likely worse but coordinate from a shift may find different local. We can include a few exact evaluates and maybe choose best shift as start (if lower than parent). However if shift worse, coordinate from it may not beat parent; but it explores. Since 512 fast, we can run coordinate on a shift if time. But stage1 limited. Maybe just evaluate shifts and use best shift as start if better; not run coordinate from all.

For larger n, starts: best resample, one smooth random resample from random pattern, maybe best shifted resample. But random may waste.

Now, let's think about using the parent's exact length 512. If `height_sequence_1` sum maybe not 1, normalize. If `height_sequence_1` has zeros, log perturb need handle zeros; use `np.maximum(a,1e-12)`.

Helper `_as_1d_nonneg_norm`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a)&(a>0),a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=0 or not math.isfinite(s): return np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size)
    a=a/s
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s2=a.sum()
    if s2<=0: return ...
    a=a/s2
    return a
```
If underlying zeros all, fallback uniform.

```

`_consider(seq)`:
```
def _consider(seq):
  nonlocal best_list,best_score
  try:
    a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a)&(a>=0),a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return
    a=a/s
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    lst=[float(x) for x in a]
    val=evaluate_sequence(lst)
    val=float(val)
    if math.isfinite(val) and val<best_score:
       best_score=val; best_list=lst
    # also update internal best_arr? We'll separate.
  except Exception: pass
```

`_smooth_polish(a, deadline, reserve=30)`:
```
def _smooth_polish(a, deadline, reserve=30):
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
    if n>1024: return a
    best=a; best_m= float(np.max(np.convolve(a,a)))
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    lambs = [20,60,150,400,1000,2000]
    maxiters = 60 if n<=512 else 30
    for lam in lambs:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
       # maybe shuffle? no
       def fun(z):
          zc = z - np.mean(z)
          # if range > 50, scale? 
          maxz=zc.max(); ez=np.exp(zc-maxz); a=ez/ez.sum()
          b=np.convolve(a,a); bm=float(b.max())
          sh=lam*(b-bm); ex=np.exp(sh); Z=ex.sum()
          if not finite or Z==0: return bm, np.zeros_like(z)
          f=bm + math.log(Z)/lam
          w=ex/Z
          # h = conv(w, a[::-1]) slice
          conv = np.convolve(w, a[::-1], mode='full')
          h = conv[n-1:2*n-1]
          grad_a = 2*h
          A = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
          gz = a*(grad_a - A)
          gz -= gz.mean()
          # optional clip
          gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
          return f,gz
       try:
          res = scipy_minimize(fun, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':20, 'maxfun':60, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7})
       except Exception:
          break
       z = res.x if finite else z
       z = z - z.mean()
       e=np.exp(z-z.max()); a2=e/e.sum()
       m2=float(np.max(np.convolve(a2,a2)))
       if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
          best_m=m2; best=a2
          z = z  # continue from new
       else:
          # optionally continue with current z (not best)
          pass
       z = np.log(np.maximum(best,a small))? Maybe reset z to best for next λ to ensure.
       z = np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    return best
```
L-BFGS options maxfun 60 per λ; 6 λ =>360 fun. For n=512 maybe okay. If no improvement, still cost. We can stop if time. But `minimize` cannot time-interrupt; maxfun small. Good.

Is gradient correct for logsumexp stable? `f=bm + log(Z)/lam`. `Z=sum exp(lam(b-bm))`; derivative of f? As discussed, correct. But note `b` depends on a, and bm in shift cancels. We don't need derivative of bm. Good.

Potential issue: `np.convolve(w, a[::-1])` length 3n-2; slice `n-1:2*n-1` length n. For n=512, okay. But this computes h_i = ∑ a_j w_{i+j}, yes. Let's verify using previous: w length L, ar len n, conv index i+n-1. Our slice n-1:2n-1 endpoints: indices n-1..2n-2 inclusive. Good.

Now, local refine candidate intervals. Let's implement elegantly:
```
def _min_coord(...):
    ...
    # grid vals
    best_x = float(x0); best_f = ev(best_x)
    idx_min = int(np.argmin(vals))
    if vals[idx_min] < best_f:
       best_x=float(xs[idx_min]); best_f=float(vals[idx_min])
    # candidate indices
    cand=[]
    def add_idx(idx):
       idx=int(idx)
       if 0<=idx<g and idx not in cand_set: cand_set.add(idx); cand.append(idx)
    add_idx(idx_min); add_idx(0); add_idx(g-1)
    cell = int(np.searchsorted(xs, x0)) # x0 point index
    # since xs includes 0,1 linear, cell = round(x0*(g-1))
    for d in range(-2,3): add_idx(cell+d)
    order = np.argsort(vals)
    for idx in order[:max(1, refine+2)]: add_idx(idx)
    for f in (0.25,0.5,0.75): add_idx(int(round(f*(g-1))))
    # limit by vals
    if len(cand) > max(2, refine+4):
       cand_set_ordered = sorted(cand, key=lambda ii: vals[ii])[:refine+4]
       cand = cand_set_ordered
    # refine
    for idx in cand:
       if idx==0: lo,hi=0.0,xs[1]
       elif idx==g-1: lo,hi=xs[-2],1.0
       else: lo,hi=xs[idx-1],xs[idx+1]
       # local mini grid
       if hi-lo <= 1e-12: x=lo
       else:
          mids = np.linspace(lo,hi,local_pts)
          # vectorized mids eval? Use scalar for local_pts small
          fm = [ev(float(x)) for x in mids]
          im=argmin
          lo2 = mids[im-1] if im>0 else lo

def _golden_min(ev, lo, hi, iters):
    if hi <= lo:
        x=lo; return x, ev(x)
    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    a=lo; b=hi
    # include endpoints and maybe midpoint
    best_x=lo; best_f=ev(a)
    f_b=ev(b); if f_b<best_f: best...
    c=b-gr*(b-a); d=a+gr*(b-a)
    fc=ev(c); fd=ev(d); update
    for _ in range(iters):
       if fc < fd:
          b=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=b-gr*(b-a); fc=ev(c)
       else:
          a=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=a+gr*(b-a); fd=ev(d)
       # update with c,d maybe only new
       if fc < best_f: best_x=c; best_f=fc
       if fd < best_f: best_x=d; best_f=fd
    xm=0.5*(a+b); fx=ev(xm); update.
    return best_x,best_f
```

Code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception: linprog=None
try: from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception: scipy_minimize=None

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a>0.0), a, 0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a=a/s
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s2=float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300: return np.full(a.size,1.0/float(a.size))
    a=a/s2
    return a
```
If input negative true nonneg? We clip >0.

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=_normalize(a)
    if a.size==0: return float('inf')
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    m=float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*a.size*m)
```
Since sum=1.

`_pick_params`:
```
def _pick_params(n):
  if n<=64: return np.linspace(0,1,33),5,22,5
  if n<=128: ...

`_random_smooth`: Use `scale` maybe 0.5. Must produce positive; after max. For large n, random smooth may be too spiky if exponent. Use z std moderate. Code:
```
def _random_smooth(n, rng, scale=0.45):
    t=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    maxk=min(16, n//2)
    for k in range(1,maxk+1):
       amp = scale/(1.0+0.75*k)
       z += amp*(rng.normal()*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t/n) + rng.normal()*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*t/n))
    z += 0.05*rng.normal(size=n)/math.sqrt(max(1,n))? optional
    z -= z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -4.0, 4.0)
    a=np.exp(z); a/=a.sum()
    return _normalize(a)
```
This keeps positive with exp clip range e^-4..e^4. Good.

```
def _lp_improve(q, deadline, reserve=30, steps=4, per_time=5):
    if linprog is None: return q
    q=_normalize(q); n=q.size
    if n > 640: return q
    b=np.convolve(q,q); m=float(b.max()); best=q; best_m=m
    for _ in range(steps):
       if time > deadline-reserve: break
       rem = max(0.5, deadline - reserve - time.now())
       tl = min(per_time, rem/3.0)
       if tl < 0.2: break
       M = m*(1.0+1e-8)+1e-12
       A=_build_conv_matrix(q)
       g=_lp_solve_one(q,M,A,tl)
       if g is None: break
       # candidates
       cand_best=best; cand_m=best_m; cand_q=best
       for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
           c=(1-t)q+t*g; c=_normalize(c)
           mb=float(np.max(np.convolve(c,c)))
           if mb < cand_m - 1e-13:
              cand_m=mb; cand_q=c; cand_best=c
       if cand_m < best_m - 1e-13:
          best=cand_best; q=cand_q; m=cand_m
       else:
          break
    return best
```

def _record_fast(a):
  nonlocal best_arr,best_fast
  try:
     arr=_normalize(a); sc=_fast_score(arr)
     if math.isfinite(sc) and (best_arr is None or sc < best_fast - 1e-12):

```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    scipy_minimize = None

_GOLD = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s2
    return a


def _fast_score(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * float(a.size) * m)


def _pick_params(n):
    if n <= 64:
        return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 33), 5, 22, 5
    if n <= 128:
        return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 49), 5, 20, 5
    if n <= 256:
        return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 65), 5, 20, 4
    if n <= 512:
        return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 81), 5, 20, 4
    if n <= 1024:
        return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 57), 4, 17, 4
    if n <= 2048:
        return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 33), 3, 15, 3
    return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 25), 2, 13, 3


def _golden_min(ev, lo, hi, iters):
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi <= lo:
        x = lo
        return x, float(ev(x))

    best_x = lo
    best_f = float(ev(lo))
    fb = float(ev(hi))
    if fb < best_f:
        best_x, best_f = hi, fb

    a = lo
    b = hi
    c = b - _GOLD * (b - a)
    d = a + _GOLD * (b - a)
    fc = float(ev(c))
    fd = float(ev(d))
    if fc < best_f:
        best_x, best_f = c, fc
    if fd < best_f:
        best_x, best_f = d, fd

    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        if fc < fd:
            b = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = b - _GOLD * (b - a)
            fc = float(ev(c))
            if fc < best_f:
                best_x, best_f = c, fc
        else:
            a = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = a + _GOLD * (b - a)
            fd = float(ev(d))
            if fd < best_f:
                best_x, best_f = d, fd

    xm = 0.5 * (a + b)
    fx = float(ev(xm))
    if fx < best_f:
        best_x, best_f = xm, fx
    return best_x, best_f


def _min_coord(Cslice, add, i_in_slice, S0, x0, outmax,
               grid_pts, refine, iters, local_pts):
    g = grid_pts.size
    n = add.size
    xs = grid_pts

    def ev(x):
        x = float(x)
        if x < 0.0:
            x = 0.0
        elif x > 1.0:
            x = 1.0
        o = 1.0 - x
        sc0 = o * o
        arr = Cslice * sc0
        sc1 = x * o / S0
        if sc1 != 0.0:
            arr += sc1 * add
        arr[i_in_slice] += x * x
        vi = float(arr.max())
        vo = float(outmax * sc0)
        return vi if vi > vo else vo

    x0c = float(x0)
    if x0c < 0.0:
        x0c = 0.0
    elif x0c > 1.0:
        x0c = 1.0

    best_x = x0c
    best_f = float(ev(x0c))

    sc0g = (1.0 - xs) * (1.0 - xs)
    sc1g = xs * (1.0 - xs) / S0
    tmp = Cslice[None, :] * sc0g[:, None] + add[None, :] * sc1g[:, None]
    tmp[:, i_in_slice] += xs * xs
    vals_in = tmp.max(axis=1)
    vals = np.maximum(vals_in, outmax * sc0g)

    idx_min = int(np.argmin(vals))
    if float(vals[idx_min]) < best_f - 1e-15:
        best_x = float(xs[idx_min])
        best_f = float(vals[idx_min])

    cand_set = set()
    cand_list = []

    def add_cand(idx):
        idx = int(idx)
        if 0 <= idx < g and idx not in cand_set:
            cand_set.add(idx)
            cand_list.append(idx)

    add_cand(idx_min)
    add_cand(0)
    add_cand(g - 1)

    cell = int(np.clip(x0c * (g - 1), 0, g - 1))
    for dd in (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2):
        add_cand(cell + dd)

    order = np.argsort(vals)
    for idx in order[: max(1, int(refine) + 2)]:
        add_cand(idx)

    for frac in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
        add_cand(int(round(frac * (g - 1))))

    limit = max(3, int(refine) + 4)
    if len(cand_list) > limit:
        cand_list = sorted(cand_list, key=lambda ii: float(vals[ii]))[:limit]

    lp = max(3, int(local_pts))
    for idx in cand_list:
        if idx == 0:
            lo, hi = 0.0, float(xs[1]) if g > 1 else 1.0
        elif idx == g - 1:
            lo, hi = float(xs[-2]) if g > 1 else 0.0, 1.0
        else:
            lo, hi = float(xs[idx - 1]), float(xs[idx + 1])

        if hi - lo <= 1e-13:
            fx = float(ev(0.5 * (lo + hi)))
            if fx < best_f:
                best_x, best_f = 0.5 * (lo + hi), fx
            continue

        mids = np.linspace(lo, hi, lp)
        fms = [float(ev(float(xx))) for xx in mids]
        im = int(np.argmin(fms))
        if fms[im] < best_f:
            best_x, best_f = float(mids[im]), float(fms[im])

        lo2 = float(mids[im - 1]) if im > 0 else lo
        hi2 = float(mids[im + 1]) if im < lp - 1 else hi
        if hi2 - lo2 <= 1e-13 or (hi2 - lo2) < 0.05 * (hi - lo):
            lo2, hi2 = lo, hi

        xg, fg = _golden_min(ev, lo2, hi2, iters)
        if fg < best_f:
            best_x, best_f = xg, fg

    return best_x, best_f


def _coordinate_decent(a, deadline, reserve=30.0, max_sweeps=10000,
                       max_perturb=3, rng=None):
    a = _normalize(a).copy()
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return a, float("inf")

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = m
    grid_pts, refine, iters, local_pts = _pick_params(n)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    perturb_scales = [0.025, 0.055, 0.11, 0.2, 0.32][: max(0, int(max_perturb))]
    perturb_count = 0
    no_improve = 0

    for sweep in range(max(1, int(max_sweeps))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve:
            break

        if sweep % 2 == 0:
            order = range(n)
        else:
            order = range(n - 1, -1, -1)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve:
                break

            x0 = float(a[i])
            S0 = 1.0 - x0
            if S0 <= 1e-10:
                continue

            S2 = S0 * S0
            C = b / S2
            add = 2.0 * a
            add[i] = 0.0
            C[i:i + n] -= (x0 / S2) * add
            diag = 2 * i
            C[diag] -= x0 * x0 / S2

            mn = float(C.min())
            if mn < 0.0:
                np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

            outmax = 0.0
            if i > 0:
                om = float(np.max(C[:i]))
                if om > outmax:
                    outmax = om
            end = i + n
            if end < L:
                om = float(np.max(C[end:]))
                if om > outmax:
                    outmax = om

            x_new, f_new = _min_coord(
                C, add, i, S0, x0, outmax,
                grid_pts, refine, iters, local_pts
            )

            if math.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < m - 1e-13:
                factor = (1.0 - x_new) / S0
                if factor < 0.0:
                    factor = 0.0
                a[i] = 0.0
                a *= factor
                a[i] = x_new

                sc0 = (1.0 - x_new) * (1.0 - x_new)
                sc1 = x_new * (1.0 - x_new) / S0
                b = C * sc0
                b[i:i + n] += sc1 * add
                b[diag] += x_new * x_new

                mb = float(b.max())
                if math.isfinite(mb) and mb < m:
                    m = mb
                    if m < best_m - 1e-14:
                        best_m = m
                        best_a = a.copy()

        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a /= s
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        m = float(b.max())
        if not math.isfinite(m):
            a = best_a.copy()
            b = np.convolve(a, a)
            m = float(b.max())
            break

        if m < best_m - 1e-14:
            best_m = m
            best_a = a.copy()

        if best_m - m < 1e-12 and sweep > 4:
            no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve = 0

        if no_improve >= 2:
            if perturb_count < len(perturb_scales) and time.monotonic() < deadline - reserve - 35.0:
                scale = float(perturb_scales[perturb_count])
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12)) + scale * rng.normal(size=n)
                z -= z.mean()
                e = np.exp(z - z.max())
                ap = _normalize(e)
                mp = float(np.max(np.convolve(ap, ap)))
                if math.isfinite(mp) and mp < best_m * 1.25 + 1e-12:
                    a = ap
                    b = np.convolve(a, a)
                    m = float(mp)
                    no_improve = 0
                    perturb_count += 1
                    continue
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    b = np.convolve(a, a)
                    m = float(b.max())
                    break
            elif len(sweeps) if False else True:
                break

    return best_a, float(2.0 * n * best_m)


def _resample(a, new_n):
    new_n = int(new_n)
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(new_n, 1.0 / float(max(1, new_n)), dtype=np.float64)

    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=False)
    fp = np.concatenate([a, [a[0]]])
    xp2 = np.concatenate([xp, [1.0]])
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(xn, xp2, fp)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out) & (out > 0.0), out, 0.0)
    return _normalize(out)


def _random_smooth(n, rng, scale=0.45):
    n = int(n)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    maxk = min(16, max(1, n // 2))
    two_pi = 2.0 * math.pi
    for k in range(1, maxk + 1):
        amp = scale / (1.0 + 0.75 * float(k))
        z += amp * (
            rng.normal() * np.cos(two_pi * k * t / float(n)) +
            rng.normal() * np.sin(two_pi * k * t / float(n))
        )
    z += 0.06 * rng.normal(size=n)
    z = np.clip(z - z.mean(), -4.5, 4.5)
    return _normalize(np.exp(z))


def _build_conv_matrix(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    n = q.size
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for t in range(n):
        A[t, :t + 1] = q[t::-1]
    for t in range(n, 2 * n - 1):
        lo = t - n + 1
        A[t, lo:] = q[t - lo:lo - 1:-1]
    return A


def _lp_solve_one(q, M, A, time_limit):
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    c = -np.ones(q.size, dtype=np.float64) - 1e-4 * q
    bub = np.full(A.shape[0], float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = (0.0, 100.0)

    opt_lists = []
    if time_limit is not None and time_limit > 0.05:
        opt_lists.append({
            "presolve": True,
            "output_flag": False,
            "time_limit": float(max(0.05, time_limit)),
        })
    opt_lists.append({"presolve": True, "output_flag": False})
    opt_lists.append({})

    methods = ["highs"]
    if q.size <= 300:
        methods.append("simplex")

    for meth in methods:
        for opts in opt_lists:
            try:
                res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, bounds=bounds,
                              method=meth, options=opts.copy())
            except Exception:
                continue
            x = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if x is None:
                continue
            x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
            if x.size != q.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                continue
            if np.any(x < -1e-7):
                continue
            try:
                Ab = A @ x
                if np.any(Ab > M + 1e-5):
                    continue
            except Exception:
                continue
            x = np.where(x > 0.0, x, 0.0)
            s = float(x.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
                continue
            return x / s
    return None


def _lp_improve(q, deadline, reserve=30.0, steps=4, per_time=5.0):
    if linprog is None:
        return _normalize(q)
    q = _normalize(q)
    n = q.size
    if n > 700:
        return q

    b = np.convolve(q, q)
    m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return q

    best_q = q.copy()
    best_m = m

    for _ in range(max(1, int(steps))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - reserve - time.monotonic())
        tl = min(float(per_time), rem / 3.0)
        if tl < 0.25:
            break

        M = m * (1.0 + 1e-8) + 1e-13
        try:
            A = _build_conv_matrix(q)
        except Exception:
            break
        g = _lp_solve_one(q, M, A, tl)
        if g is None:
            break

        cand_q = best_q
        cand_m = best_m
        for tfrac in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            cand = _normalize((1.0 - tfrac) * q + tfrac * g)
            cb = np.convolve(cand, cand)
            cm = float(cb.max())
            if math.isfinite(cm) and cm < cand_m - 1e-13:
                cand_m = cm
                cand_q = cand

        if cand_m < best_m - 1e-13:
            best_q = cand_q
            best_m = cand_m
            q = cand_q
            b = np.convolve(q, q)
            m = float(b.max())
        else:
            break

    return best_q


def _smooth_polish(a, deadline, reserve=30.0):
    if scipy_minimize is None:
        return _normalize(a)
    a = _normalize(a).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n > 1024:
        return a

    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    best_m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(best_m):
        return a
    best_a = a.copy()

    lambs = (30.0, 100.0, 350.0, 1000.0)
    for lam in lambs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve:
            break
        z = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
        z = z - z.mean()

        def fun(zarr):
            zc = np.asarray(zarr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            zc = np.where(np.isfinite(zc), zc, 0.0)
            zc -= zc.mean()
            zc = np.clip(zc, -80.0, 80.0)
            ez = np.exp(zc - zc.max())
            s = float(ez.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                return 1e100, np.zeros_like(zc)
            aa = ez / s

            bb = np.convolve(aa, aa)
            bm = float(bb.max())
            if not math.isfinite(bm):
                return 1e100, np.zeros_like(zc)

            sh = lam * (bb - bm)
            ex = np.exp(sh)
            Z = float(ex.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
                return bm, np.zeros_like(zc)
            fval = bm + math.log(Z) / lam
            w = ex / Z

            conv_w = np.convolve(w, aa[::-1], mode="full")
            h = conv_w[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
            grad_a = 2.0 * h
            A = float(np.dot(aa, grad_a))
            gz = aa * (grad_a - A)
            gz -= gz.mean()
            gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
            return fval, gz

        try:
            res = scipy_minimize(
                fun, z, jac=True, method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": 28, "maxfun": 100,
                         "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-7, "maxls": 20}
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        znew = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if znew is None:
            znew = z
        znew = np.asarray(znew, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        znew = np.where(np.isfinite(znew), znew, 0.0)
        znew -= znew.mean()
        ez = np.exp(znew - znew.max())
        s = float(ez.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            break
        a2 = _normalize(ez)
        m2 = float(np.max(np.convolve(a2, a2)))
        if math.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m - 1e-13:
            best_m = m2
            best_a = a2
            z = znew
        else:
            z = znew

    return best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = max(5.0, float(budget_s))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget_s
    deadline = t0 + max(20.0, budget_s - 80.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_arr = None
    best_fast = float("inf")
    best_list = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    q4 = np.array([0.2, 0.4, 0.2, 0.2], dtype=np.float64)

    def _record_fast(a):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_fast
        try:
            arr = _normalize(a)
            if arr.size == 0:
                return arr
            b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
            sc = float(2.0 * arr.size * b.max())
            if math.isfinite(sc) and (best_arr is None or sc < best_fast - 1e-12):
                best_fast = sc
                best_arr = arr.copy()
            return arr
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _record_exact(a):
        nonlocal best_list, best_exact, best_arr, best_fast
        try:
            arr = _normalize(a)
            lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if not math.isfinite(val):
                return
            if val < best_exact:
                best_exact = val
                best_list = lst
            if val < best_fast - 1e-12:
                best_fast = val
                best_arr = arr.copy()
            elif best_arr is None:
                best_fast = val
                best_arr = arr.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    _record_exact(q4)

    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    base_n = 512
    p = None
    if parent is not None:
        p = _normalize(parent)
        _record_exact(p)
        if p.size >= 16:
            base_n = int(p.size)
            shifts = [1, -1, 2, -2, 4, -4, 8, -8, 16, -16,
                      32, -32, 64, -64, 128, -128, 255, -255]
            for sft in shifts:
                if abs(sft) < p.size:
                    rr = np.roll(p, int(sft))
                    _record_fast(rr)
                    _record_exact(rr)

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = _normalize(q4)
        best_fast = _fast_score(best_arr)

    if budget_s > 1000:
        reserve_rest = 450.0
    elif budget_s > 600:
        reserve_rest = 250.0
    elif budget_s > 300:
        reserve_rest = 120.0
    else:
        reserve_rest = 40.0

    stage1_end = max(
        t0 + 10.0,
        min(deadline - 30.0, t0 + 900.0, deadline - reserve_rest)
    )

    start_q = _resample(best_arr, base_n)
    _record_fast(start_q)

    if best_arr is not None and best_arr.size != base_n:
        start_q = _resample(best_arr, base_n)

    q_opt = _coordinate_decent(start_q, stage1_end, reserve=20.0,
                               max_sweeps=20000, max_perturb=4, rng=rng)
    _record_fast(q_opt)
    _record_exact(q_opt)

    if time.monotonic() < stage1_end - 70.0 and budget_s > 400:
        q_rand = _random_smooth(base_n, rng, scale=0.40)
        scr = _fast_score(q_rand)
        if math.isfinite(scr) and scr < best_fast + 0.25:
            end_rand = min(stage1_end, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
            q_rand_opt = _coordinate_decent(q_rand, end_rand, reserve=15.0,
                                            max_sweeps=10000, max_perturb=2, rng=rng)
            _record_fast(q_rand_opt)
            _record_exact(q_rand_opt)

    if best_arr is not None and best_arr.size <= 512 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        end_smooth = min(deadline - 180.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
        q_sm = _smooth_polish(best_arr, end_smooth, reserve=25.0)
        _record_fast(q_sm)
        _record_exact(q_sm)
        end_coord = min(deadline - 150.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
        q_cp = _coordinate_decent(q_sm, end_coord, reserve=15.0,
                                  max_sweeps=5000, max_perturb=2, rng=rng)
        _record_fast(q_cp)
        _record_exact(q_cp)

    if best_arr is not None and best_arr.size <= 560 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        end_lp = min(deadline - 140.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
        q_lp = _lp_improve(best_arr, end_lp, reserve=25.0, steps=3, per_time=5.0)
        _record_fast(q_lp)
        _record_exact(q_lp)
        if best_arr is not None and best_arr.size <= 560 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 110.0:
            end_c2 = min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
            q_c2 = _coordinate_decent(q_lp, end_c2, reserve=15.0,
                                      max_sweeps=3000, max_perturb=1, rng=rng)
            _record_fast(q_c2)
            _record_exact(q_c2)

    larger_dims = (1024, 2048, 4096)
    for dim in larger_dims:
        if best_arr is None:
            break
        if int(best_arr.size) >= dim:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 160.0:
            break

        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - 130.0 - time.monotonic())
        alloc = min(remaining, 520.0)
        if alloc < 130.0:
            continue

        q0 = _resample(best_arr, dim)
        _record_fast(q0)

        if alloc > 350.0:
            q_rand = _random_smooth(dim, rng, scale=0.42)
            _record_fast(q_rand)

        end_dim = min(deadline - 110.0, time.monotonic() + alloc - 30.0)
        q_opt_dim = _coordinate_decent(q0, end_dim, reserve=25.0, 100000,
                                       max_perturb=2 if dim <= 1024 else 1, rng=rng)
        _record_fast(q_opt_dim)
        _record_exact(q_opt_dim)

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 140.0 and dim <= 1024:
            end_sm2 = min(deadline - 120.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
            q_sm2 = _smooth_polish(best_arr, end_sm2, reserve=20.0)
            _record_fast(q_sm2)
            _record_exact(q_sm2)

    if best_arr is not None:
        _record_exact(best_arr)

    if best_list is None or not all(math.isfinite(float(x)) for x in best_list) or len(best_list) == 0:
        best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]

    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]
    except Exception:
        best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]

    return [float(x) for x in best_list]
```
